--- Plausibility: does the target behave like its climate? ---
  Rules of thumb, not arithmetic. CHECK means look at it; a real result can
  break a rule of thumb. Ensemble = mean over the models with both scenarios.
  Thresholds are judgment calls, stated with each check.
  Each check runs once per target scenario, over the models that target
  shares with the reference. The expected signs assume a target warmer than
  the reference; for a cooler one (ssp126 against ssp245) they reverse, so
  CHECK flags there are expected.

  ===== target ssp126 vs reference ssp245 =====

  P1. Temperature: ensemble population-weighted mean temperature,
      target minus reference (C), at the years in --temp_years.
      Expect about 0 in 2022 (the scenarios share history to 2014 and
      differ little by the 2020s), then positive and growing. CHECK if
      2040 or 2050 is <= 0, if the last year is not the largest, or if
      any value exceeds 2 C.
